## 01:08–01:14 CDT — STONKCOIN launch investigation (pending full trace)
- **Correction/update:** user clarified that `0xf9b993d5e1d834a65cb7fe3dce480287b55d8241` is the wallet and `0xa37d551f2a768af49ec5a1df68efbfac91971bd3` is the contract. Direct `eth_getCode` confirms this on Robinhood Chain: `0xf9b993…8241` is an EOA; `0xa37d55…1bd3` has deployed bytecode (~9,096 hex chars). Both addresses are EOAs/no-code on Base and Ethereum.
- Direct Robinhood Chain calls identify `0xa37d55…1bd3` as **StonkCoin (STONKCOIN)**, 18 decimals. `totalSupply()` returned 20,889,100 STONKCOIN during the check; GeckoTerminal reported normalized supply 21,000,000. The wallet's onchain `balanceOf` was 70,000.59409155954 STONKCOIN.
- The contract first had code around Robinhood block 84,772,240 (about 06:01 UTC / 01:01 CDT). The exact deployment/factory transaction is not yet resolved; the full Blockscout browser trace remains running and must be treated as **pending**, not completed.
- Wallet snapshot from direct RPC: ~0.0053956769 native ETH on Robinhood Chain with 14 sent transactions; ~0.0000225917 ETH on Ethereum with 3 sent transactions; zero native balance/nonce on Base. It is not yet verified who funded the wallet or where proceeds went.
- Market snapshot from DexScreener/GeckoTerminal: Uniswap v4 STONKCOIN/NVDA pool ID `0x58714a8820176196130caa0ccefc45079ac47fc73c2f6700079b2dfc62306c8a`; price about $0.00392, FDV about $82.4k, liquidity about $8.67k, ~152 buys/95 sells and ~$38.18k volume at first sight. DexScreener pool creation timestamp corresponds to roughly 01:10 CDT.
- Contract-level `eth_getLogs` returned zero events from first-code block through latest even though `balanceOf` and trading data show balances/activity. This is an **observed anomaly**, not yet proof of malicious intent; do not repeat the earlier interpretive claim that invisible movement was definitively deliberate until the full trace/implementation path is verified.
- User's assessment: “Smells very fishy this whole launch.” Trading authority remains closed; no buy/sell/swap, wallet connection, signature, or fund movement was authorized or performed.

## 01:25 CDT — STONKCOIN investigation COMPLETE (replaces the pending 01:08–01:14 trace)
- **CORRECTION:** the earlier direct-RPC inference that STONKCOIN deployed around 01:01 CDT and had zero Transfer events was wrong because the Robinhood RPC lacks the needed historical state/log coverage. Blockscout verified the contract deployed **2026-10-09 20:53:44 CDT** and has normal indexed transfers, **69 holders**, 18 decimals, and 20,889,100 circulating/reported supply at inspection. Do not repeat the “invisible transfers / deliberate hiding” claim.
- `0xf9b993d5e1d834a65cb7fe3dce480287b55d8241` is an EOA/trader wallet; `0xa37d551f2a768af49ec5a1df68efbfac91971bd3` is the verified **StonkCoin (STONKCOIN)** ERC-20 on Robinhood Chain. The wallet is **not the deployer**, and the completed trace found no direct onchain link between it and the deployer.
- Contract creation: tx `0xe1633fce6e97b31fc7c021272cca7e7ea46edba3afac5343a339f9a7c31ebe30`; deployer EOA `0x0eB3AdCcDD8a057776a446c6ef409Ea1aDDDA2e2`; factory `0xDeeD5aeB1B23EDaaeBb75D1ee61aC2259a6209E4`. Creation minted 18,900 STONK to MiningEngine `0x767698444949B96014D6B1FA7f8D24431d5d7993` and 2,100 STONK to Genesis `0x72Afd72846B61b6E9fE84253Fb784cb65873866D`.
- Wallet funding trail: 1.0 ETH arrived on Ethereum from Binance hot wallet `0x9696f59E4d72E237BE84fFD425DCaD154Bf96976`, then 0.9995998588 ETH was bridged through MetaMask MetaBridge; 0.9907906005 ETH arrived on Robinhood Chain via SpokePool.
- Before buying STONK, the wallet sent 0.3 ETH each to `0x49e1DAAf707828951F25E3F95798EC2CB9bd1ECd` and `0x88D0187703fC29119c53972dbd50C5778c4778Be`; both later appeared among STONK approvers, suggesting related traders but not proving common ownership.
- Trading trace: wallet bought **305,050.5941 STONK for 0.3904897439 ETH**, then sold **235,050 STONK** across six swaps for about **1.66914 ETH**, retaining about **70,000.5941 STONK**. Approximate realized gain on the sold portion was ~1.28 ETH before gas/bridge costs (~4.3× gross proceeds vs initial buy cost).
- Proceeds route: bridged 1.2999959207 ETH and later 0.3599959579 ETH back to Ethereum; bridge payouts were 1.2858812314 ETH and 0.3559168066 ETH. It then sent **1.28 ETH + 0.3657008937 ETH = 1.6457008937 ETH** to consolidation wallet `0x4B27952B2579705F82b255d4680F6B748E7399F1`. A final 0.004 ETH bridge also occurred.
- Market snapshot during investigation: STONKCOIN/NVDA Uniswap v4 pool ID `0x58714a8820176196130caa0ccefc45079ac47fc73c2f6700079b2dfc62306c8a`, about $0.00392/token, ~$82.4k FDV, ~$8.67k liquidity, ~152 buys/95 sells and ~$38.18k volume. High churn on thin liquidity plus apparently related early traders are yellow flags, but the collected evidence does **not** prove the trader wallet belongs to the team or that the launch is a scam.

- Verified the `AGNTS by tut` collection on Robinhood Chain (NFT contract `0x57eFd86cf3ab1812CB47F0FD3c3d5bf0eFb1F2D5`) and its holder-reward mechanics through mint.agnt.social, docs.agnt.social, OpenSea listings, and onchain reads. - Investigated OpenSea item `DX Terminal / NyTransKid #2113` on Base at contract `0x41dc69132cce31fcbf6755c84538ca268520246f`. Onchain `ownerOf(2113)` resolves to the user’s known square wallet.
- Contract inspection showed a conventional ERC-721-style gaming/mint contract (`DX Terminal`, symbol `TERMINAL`) with mint/admin-mint, mint timing/price, payment recipient, pause, base URI, approval, transfer, ownership, and metadata functions. No agent-wallet, capabilities, autonomy, ERC-8004, or other 721T agent machinery was found. Conclusion: this item is a game collectible, not a Looper-style 721T agent NFT.

## 2026-10-10 05:43 CDT — OpenSea 721T search correction
- **Correction to the immediately preceding assistant response:** OpenSea’s live text search for `721t` did not surface a relevant 721T collection; it returned an unrelated one-item Ethereum collection named “Search.” OpenSea does not expose a verified 721T/ERC-8048 marketplace filter in the checked page, so no complete OpenSea inventory of 721T NFTs was established.
- The official draft ERC-8048 spec calls ERC-721T its Agent Metadata Profile. It uses ERC-8048’s existing `metadata(uint256,string)` interface and `MetadataSet` event rather than defining a separate Solidity interface; ERC-8048’s interface ID is `0xdf670be1`. Reserved agent keys include `context`, `endpoint[mcp]`, `endpoint[a2a]`, `endpoint[web]`, `endpoint[x402]`, chain-keyed `address[...]`, and optional `account[...][index]`.
- A direct `supportsInterface(0xdf670be1)` call to the Loopers proxy address reverted during this check. Therefore this run did **not** independently verify Loopers’ current contract as ERC-8048-compliant via that one-call test, and the prior claim that this test had confirmed it was overstated. No other OpenSea collection was verified as 721T in this search.

## Daily rabbit hole — 2026-10-10
- Discovery: The day CryptoPunks shipped with a money-stealing bug — and the community resurrected the broken originals. CryptoPunks' June 2017 V1 contract (0x6Ba6f2207e343923BA692e5Cae646Fb0F566DB8D) had a one-line bug letting buyers withdraw the seller's ETH after purchase; Larva Labs abandoned it, re-airdropped all 10,000 as V2 on June 23, 2017; the community later built ERC-721 wrappers (new wrapper 0x282bDD42f4eb70e7A9D9F40c8fEA0825B7f68C5D, 5,125 wrapped today) to safely trade the immutable V1 originals. Verified onchain: V1 contract still holds 4,131 bytes of code, wrapper 7,703 bytes, wrapper totalSupply 5,125. Sources: cryptopunksnotdead/awesome-cryptopunks-bubble (PUNKS_V1_HISTORY.md, PUNKS_V1_NOTES.md), Decrypt (2022-01-26), 0xfoobar's 2021 exploit thread, SSRN copyright paper. - **CORRECTION to the DX Terminal 721T rationale recorded earlier today:** The conclusion remains that DX Terminal contract `0x41dc69132cce31fcbf6755c84538ca268520246f` was not verified as ERC-721T/ERC-8048. However, absence of `agentWallet`, capabilities, or autonomy functions is not itself a valid 721T test: the official draft defines 721T as an ERC-8048 metadata-key profile, not a separate agent-execution interface. The checked DX Terminal contract exposed ordinary ERC-721/game-mint functions, no ERC-8048 metadata surface was found in the inspection, and `supportsInterface(0xdf670be1)` reverted.
